heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 7 releases · 6 stable · 1 prerelease
v0.8.198 Stable2026-10-25T13:12:03Z

HyperFrames v0.8.198

Released on 2026-10-25.

Cloud renders now split the work by how costly each part is to capture, so one heavy stretch no longer holds up the rest. On a heavy 4K test project that made renders about a third faster at the same cost. Snapshot also waits for slow scenes, and renders hide statically hidden elements the way preview does.

Fixes

  • CLI: Snapshot waits for a slow scene to finish building instead of saving a stale frame (6b424e02c4987, #6290).
  • Core: Hide static data-hidden elements in render as preview does (36b2c3c2b9a48, #6296).
  • Producer: Refuse private and non-HTTPS previewUrl fetches (683e9b1765aa1, #6297).

Performance

  • Producer: Cut distributed chunks by estimated capture cost (aae32e43eddb3, #6295).

Docs & Examples

  • Start: The Studio app first, the browser editor named, a showcase wall that renders (f5ab7711fb8d0, #6303).

Internal

  • Release: Keep the packed release tarballs as an artifact on release pull requests (e938e53ea6952, #6292).

Full changelog

v0.8.197 Stable2026-10-25T09:42:42Z

HyperFrames v0.8.197

Released on 2026-10-25.

Cloud renders of opaque VP9 video use compressed frames instead of lossless ones, which cuts their cost by about 40 percent. Snapshot reports a clip it cannot read instead of saving a black frame, and Studio frees each closed project's memory.

Features

  • Lint: Warn when a round-capped line hidden by its dash settings can still show a dot (2bff65b0cd13d, #6291).

Fixes

  • Sdk: Release the parsed film when a session is disposed (495dc95acdf73, #6289).
  • CLI: Snapshot fails with the clip and time instead of a silent black frame (a7273f71478b4, #6274).
  • CLI: A check that is stopped by a signal prints the report it has so far (1f740ca21a4ab, #6282).

Performance

  • Engine: Vp9 and vp8 sources without an alpha plane extract as jpeg (382a14d73e7ab, #6284).
  • Core: Text edits stop rebuilding the preview's caches every frame (751ca755f21e1, #6276).

Internal

  • Two flaky queue tests no longer race real time or the first copy's start (4d154c6a237f4, #6283).

v0.8.196 Stable2026-10-25T07:21:50Z

HyperFrames v0.8.196

Released on 2026-10-25.

Undoing a rename of a video or audio file works again: the file comes back under its old name. The CLI's add, check and archive commands get fixes too, and cache-busted images stay visible in bundles.

Fixes

  • Studio Server: Undoing a video rename brings the file back under its old name (7bc363c87c, #6285).
  • Deps: Update esbuild for the security advisory (38afc0b013, #6277).
  • CLI: Stop 'hyperframes add' hanging when a desktop clipboard is available (221f3671f2, #6271).
  • Core: Keep cache-busted images visible in bundles (32d2d3cba0, #6264).
  • CLI: Include encoded external assets in archives (c6c8d81ba3, #6266).
  • CLI: Remove stale briefs when re-freezing recipes (f5e15b2f77, #5324).
  • CLI: Check finishes on long projects with many video clips (37f5f27290, #6270).

v0.8.194 Stable2026-10-25T02:27:24Z

HyperFrames v0.8.194

Released on 2026-10-25.

Undo and restore in Studio keep the media they delete or replace, so a history step never loses a file. Motion blur no longer blacks out videos inside sub-compositions, and around 25 smaller fixes land across the CLI, core and lint.

Features

  • Studio: The blur panel turns on events with blur (19374955c7bc8, #6255).

Fixes

  • CLI: Scope explicit preview port discovery (e7169ae85b454, #6242).
  • Studio Server: Undo and restore keep the media they delete or replace (85fb19f1e822c, #6215).
  • Parsers: Keep each keyframe array on its own timing (5b752168c14a5, #6240).
  • Core: Scene shots keep their shell after leading comments (6e77d783c7c19, #6239).
  • Engine: Concat resolves relative paths without dropping clips (17332cf0df4d5, #6238).
  • Player: Keep Next available for the final slide's reveals (680f5d7465e63, #6233).
  • Sdk: Keep added elements when reopening saved overrides (31e6ed2aed959, #6231).
  • CLI: Emit valid JSON for filtered keyframes (6a917780f5903, #6228).
  • Lint: Accept GLSL script blocks without JavaScript syntax errors (f21c3e3e7e838, #6227).
  • Producer: Report unavailable frame checks in debug logs (0c3387b8beeb7, #6200).
  • Lint: Accept final font-family descriptors without semicolons (035a5c0ae1f9f, #6085).
  • Skills: Preserve Remotion easing mappings (6a2721c68a58c, #5561).
  • Producer: Keep runtime media speed in exports (066bc51831ebb, #5528).
  • Core: Prepare split-video offsets during preview preload (abcbde0b97ad9, #5356).
  • Render: Align container Chrome with the CLI (d6c20513d1f22, #5338).
  • Core: Resolve a relative data-start inside the clip's own composition (c2a6b15f6fcc1, #5336).
  • CLI: Report parent motion in template-wrapped keyframes (d36797a3b57d6, #5331).
  • CLI: Preserve ducking gain when music starts later (ad41745ab104a, #5318).
  • CLI: Preserve distinct font URLs during capture (f9d72a3f73753, #5312).
  • CLI: Include external video posters in project archives (e5e2d6f1df036, #5309).
  • CLI: Include captured raster formats in contact sheets (bb5e0f0bcf970, #5301).
  • CLI: Respect reference image orientation in grade comparisons (39364ee4fb520, #5288).
  • CLI: Reap only orphaned browsers HyperFrames launched (f180ffca64b4b, #6251).
  • Core: Motion blur no longer blacks out videos inside sub-compositions (b97de59c84a75, #6252).
  • Core: A video that starts a cut far behind the playhead catches up in steps, not at a flat 3% (e586922cd1e0c, #6198).
  • Studio: Allow a local GSAP dependency mirror (1c14fbb542897, #6247).
  • CLI: Allow disabling orphan browser cleanup (aefca2144c200, #6213).
  • Core: Name the selector when a scoped GSAP target matches nothing (e26d330887896, #5839).
  • Producer: A busy composition no longer stalls the render waiting on probe-page reads (9aadc0b9b99d9, #6241).
  • CLI: A Chrome download that stops sending is abandoned, retried, and releases the lock (fcec26ed5f35a, #6222).

Docs & Examples

  • Protect skill edits during contributor setup (0289d96ac78ab, #5346).
  • Studio App: Show the app working, three new pages, today's UI (7224812143d52, #6223).

Catalog

  • CLI: Refresh item manifests when bypassing registry cache (9a517560a827b, #5256).

Internal

  • CLI: Cover media URL duration probes (ecd7bf4184fe1, #5330).
  • Engine: Retry the alpha probe test's temp-dir cleanup when Windows holds a file (ab57d0ee14468, #6234).

v0.8.193 Stable2026-10-24T16:49:30Z

HyperFrames v0.8.193

Released on 2026-10-24.

Cloud renders of long, cut-heavy edits finish several times faster at the same or lower cost. Audio renders work again on FFmpeg 4.4.

Fixes

  • Engine: Audio encodes on FFmpeg 4.4, which has no setts duration option (3d45d1b037860, #6219).
  • CLI: Each start sweeps the running markers of CLIs that are gone (24965372dbbec, #5790).
  • CLI: Snapshot rejects a negative --at and builds its sheet from its own frames (80be6ff039bb7, #6179).
  • CLI: Snapshot seeks into a HEVC cut whose index lands a keyframe early (3cbd98890e6f6, #6217).
  • Studio Server: A history restore refuses to remove every tracked file (0eae4946d33f3, #6175).
  • Engine: Disable Dawn's DXC on Windows so WebGPU requestDevice succeeds (3dc0b97bfdfe3, #5830).
  • Studio: A scene swap no longer keeps the previous preview window in memory (291144e87ddfe, #6207).
  • Core: Color grading builds a new WebGL context when a lost one is never restored (700187b855de3, #6211).
  • Producer: A render's page-error count includes what the capture workers saw (1ab871fb117aa, #6196).
  • Engine: Renames ride out Windows file locks instead of failing the render (0a8a78edec413, #6210).

Performance

  • AWS Lambda: Cloud renders plan without reading video bytes and start chunks sooner (da4386f0d0695, #6194).

Docs & Examples

  • Skills: Say which font faces are bundled and which are fetched (8811f0bfbf3e7, #6197).

Internal

  • CLI: The lifecycle suite lets background imports settle before it resets modules (dd1cba49de4e1, #6216).
  • The lifecycle and thumbnail suites load cold modules in a hook, not in their first test (1a238171ec558, #6212).

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v15.2.0v15.2.0 — Browser install funnel Stable2026-10-25T11:52:08Z

Highlights

Install funnel in the browser (#5031)

Fresh installs now open a browser setup flow that finds your recent work and turns it into Work, Goals and Rules before setup finishes. Existing installations keep opening My Work as before.

  1. Install — npx claude-mem install installs the detected hooks, starts the worker and opens the browser.
  2. Scan — reads the last 30 days of supported local agent history. Configured excluded projects and agent-authored prompts are left out, stored prompts are deduplicated, and private-tag cleanup and configured redaction run before anything is shown or extracted.
  3. Reveal — uploads the filtered owner prompts (gzip) to CMEM Cloud for hosted intent extraction. Anonymous installs get a private, persistent installation credential that retries and sign-in reuse. Returned goals, rules and request groups fill the local My Work snapshots. Upload and extraction errors are shown and can be retried. The pre-checked product-improvement box only changes improveProduct.
  4. Storage choice — At Home or On The Go. On The Go uses the existing pairing, login and checkout flow; delivered memory credentials are activated before checkout is marked paid, and sync-only accounts keep their provider.
  5. Finish Setup — installs any other selected local tools and can import existing memory. Extraction or import in progress is kept when a settings change restarts the worker.
  6. What's next — search saved memory and prompts, keep a result as a note, then hand off to My Work. The terminal exits successfully.

Ongoing goals and progress refresh uses an installed, logged-in agent: Claude Code gets bounded file tools and memory recall only; Codex runs with a strict read-only workspace/runtime profile (no writes, no network), with native and npm runtime layouts supported; Gemini gets prepared evidence inline with no builtin tools, hooks or YOLO mode. Every saved answer passes the worker's validators.

Setup no longer fails when Cloud leaves conversations ungrouped (#5060)

On real 30-day scans the hosted model sometimes leaves conversations out of every group. The worker used to throw Hosted extraction omitted conversation … and fail setup after a successful hosted run. Now the groups Cloud returned are saved and conversations it did not place stay undecided: they show in My Work as their own request, and the local refresh can group them later. Unknown or repeated conversation ids still fail loudly.

Requirements

  • Node ^20.19.0 || >=22.12.0.
  • Uses CMEM Cloud hosted history import and intent extraction (claude-mem-pro #353, #355, #358).

Full Changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.1.0...v15.2.0

v15.1.0 Stable2026-10-25T07:44:32Z

claude-mem 15.1.0

Highlights

  • New My Work viewer (React, live data). The localhost viewer at http://localhost:37777 is replaced by the React My Work viewer on live worker data: one card per human request, progress meters, owner goals, goal alignment, shipping velocity and the build story (#5010).
  • My Work is always live. Updates appear as they arrive; the "Show updates" button is gone. The header now reads "claude-mem", links to the Classic viewer at /viewer, and shows a GitHub stars button. An open request drawer no longer reopens by itself when its request returns to the feed (#5056).
  • Request grouping and progress review. The build story stays under its cap and the story update is told how much room it has (#5053, #5054); every agent's refresh failure is shown; finished subagents show as finished, and a subagent can be marked finished from its agent card; Claude Code subagent stops are recorded.

Server

  • Per-prompt semantic injection in server mode (#5004).
  • The configured embedding model is baked into the Docker image (#5003).

MCP

  • Opt-in loopback HTTP transport with per-session workspaces (#5028).

Fixes

  • Observer: reasoning gets its own budget and cut-off replies are reported (#5033).
  • Search: unfiltered timelines are scoped to their anchor project (#5013).
  • Worker: cross-origin loopback writes to /api/import are rejected (#5025); Edit payloads carrying new scalar flags keep being trimmed (#5027).
  • Redaction: AWS secret keys under lowercase key names, PGP private key blocks and encrypted PKCS#8 keys (#5014, #5017).
  • Security: private prompt text stays out of Goals.
  • Smart-read: tree-sitter probe output read when Bun returns a Buffer (#5024), Go type aliases (#5020), Swift extension methods (#5016), symbols ending at column 0 (#5015).
  • Hooks: last_assistant_message mapped in the raw adapter (#5011).
  • Agent runner: npm-installed codex and gemini are found and started.

Tests

  • Suite split into fast and integration lanes; slow, failing and source-grep tests cut (#5032).

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v5.1.47 Stable2026-10-25T11:01:21Z
  • Memory path-state recovery and commit hooks now keep literal Git lookups working when global glob or case-insensitive pathspec modes are enabled (#11130, #11167). (batch #11187)

A user Stop pauses the workflow run it started instead of cancelling it. DAG nodes, workpool workers and task children hold where they are; a message to the paused run or Resume continues it from where it stopped. (#10808, fixes #6752) (batch #11187)

Concurrent file touches show each project memory nudge once. Project memory reads reserve the nudge before asynchronous loading, retry failed reads, and discard stale results after compaction or session changes. (#11165, addresses item 1 of #10927) (batch #11187)

A finished workflow run now shows as a finished run. When a workflow (DAG) run started from a session completes, including one that completes after recovery, the engine records the session's completion as a finished run instead of a plain reply (#11155). (batch #11187)

A finished ulw-execute plan now shows as a finished run. When this session's own ulw-execute work is marked completed, the engine records the session's completion as a finished run instead of a plain reply. Work finished by an earlier run, abandoned work, and another session's work are not counted (#11174). (batch #11187)

A completed ulw-loop plan now shows as a finished run. When this session's ulw-loop plan reaches its aggregate completion, the engine records the session's completion as a finished run instead of a plain reply. A plan that was already complete when the session started is not counted (#11175). (batch #11187)

CI exposes the lint ratchet as its own required-free status check. Pull requests, pushes and merge groups run Lint ratchet independently of the heavy-validation typecheck path. (#11128, thanks @Dante-dan) (batch #11187)

The 10k parked-backlog memory test now fails if it observed no entry reads. (#11164, part of #11091, thanks @Dante-dan) (batch #11187)

omo.dev/download no longer answers 404. Desktop builds link it from the reinstall screen and the .deb update pill; while the site's own download page is off, /download and /<locale>/download now redirect to the public beta download page at https://get.omo.dev/desktop/beta/. (#11270)

Gateway wakes, UI answers and releases reach a host session known only by its durable id. A multi-session host refuses any command that names no session, so a wake for a session read from disk or found by thread locate was lost with missing_session_id. The client now looks the session up on its host by durable id and routes the command; when no live session holds it, the delivery stays queued for the session's next start and omo thread report says the completion's wake did not reach it. (#11188, fixes #11185, part of #10995) (#11252)

Turning telemetry off through an environment variable no longer deletes summaries another session queued under a profile. A session whose OMO_PROFILE turns telemetry on could lose its queued usage summary when another session started with DO_NOT_TRACK and the base omo.json setting was off. Each summary queued from now on remembers the config it was recorded under and is judged by it when it is sent, resumed or cleaned up; summaries queued before the upgrade keep the old reading until they expire. (#11198) (#11202) (#11252)

The telemetry reference now states exactly which queued summaries an opt-out deletes and what the legacy rollups cleanup can and cannot remove. (#11181) (#11252)

senpi's on-demand memory report now shows OmO's task children and memory recall. With SENPI_MEMORY_REPORT=1, a report gains an omoTask section (resident task children, cached task records and their bytes, open event logs) and an omoRecall section (loaded memory corpora, their text bytes, the recall index's term arrays and dictionary size), so a large session's memory can be traced to these layers. Nothing is measured unless a report is taken. (#11178, part of #9442) (#11252)

Kimi K3.1 gets its own Sisyphus, Sisyphus-Junior and Atlas prompts. Kimi K3.1 Pro ids (k3.1-pro, kimi-k3.1-pro and their Highspeed versions) now route to separate copies of the K3 prompts instead of sharing K3's, and the Kimi Code id k3.1-pro gets a Kimi prompt at all. kimi-k3.1-pro maps to Kimi Code's k3.1-pro id. (#11197) (#11252)

Protocol-2 account storage has separate credential and pending-revocation namespaces. Legacy credential writes and deletes cannot overwrite either namespace; the origin key and native storage-error mappings stay shared. This is storage support for the protocol-2 rollout, not enrollment activation. (#11177, refs omo-service#628) (#11252)

A background task whose task host stays unreachable no longer comes back as a leftover session after recovery gives up. When a task child's reconnect timed out and its host answered only later, the stalled reconnect could retry after the session had already been closed and reopen it on the host, leaving a live session and its processes behind. The abandoned reconnect now stops retrying, while the parked child keeps its respawn and its single notice. (#11246, fixes #10925) (#11252)

Windows Codex commands no longer stop at a command syntax error. Generated command shims accept a quoted NODE_REPL_NODE_PATH in config.toml and also run when that setting or the config file is absent. Thanks to @fvegiard for the report and @LilMGenius for the fix. (#11222, fixes #7473) (#11252)

The ulw-research proofread lane returns edits that are checked before they land. The writing lane hands back polish-edits.json; fact polish-apply applies each edit only when it changes text nodes alone and keeps every number, hedge, qualifier, link, and claim id, fact polish-diff backstops hand edits, and fact pairs --changed-since sends every sentence polish rewrote back to the meaning readers. (#11160, part of #11009)

Changelog entries live in per-PR fragments, so pull requests stop conflicting on the changelogs. A pull request that changes what ships adds exactly one file, changes/unreleased/<pr-number>.md: the paragraph that used to go under ## [Unreleased] (or ### Fixed-style bullets), plus a <!-- changes-log: <path> --> block for each entry it used to prepend to changes.md or packages/<name>/changes.md. Only the release pull request edits those files: the release workflow writes every fragment into the release section and the logs, and deletes the fragments. What counts as shipped is unchanged, and bun script/changelog-fragments-cli.ts from-diff --base origin/dev --pr <n> converts a branch that still edits the changelogs. (#10477)

Fixed

  • Commands OmO starts under Bun now see environment changes made while it runs, as they already did under Node. Before, a child started without an explicit environment got the values the process started with, so it could run with an out-of-date temp or home directory. (#11260) (#11252)

View release →

v5.1.46 Stable2026-10-25T01:05:44Z

OmO Native init-deep committed mode no longer dead-ends. Committed mode now finishes through its own pull-request handoff instead of pointing at a skill that does not ship with OmO Native, and it reports how to finish by hand when no PR can be opened. Thanks to @HolisticDeveloper for the report and @surlymochan for the fix. (#11228, fixes #9686)

The stale-reflection-lock test leaves no background writer behind when removing its temporary root. Its teardown closes the memory wiring and awaits facts reconciliation and maintenance before removal, so a late lock publication cannot recreate the directory and make the temp-leak guard blame another test. (#11225)

ulw-research ships a Markdown block model for checking Markdown and post deliverables. It splits a Markdown report into headings, paragraphs, list items, table rows, code blocks and quotes with exact text offsets, and sorts sections with the same classifier as the HTML checks; an unclosed code fence is reported instead of passing silently, and no check uses it yet. The HTML checks now also treat a section headed exactly "Closing" as the closing section. (#11153, part of #11009)

ulw-research ships checked polish edits and a polish diff for research reports. Polish changes text nodes only: tags, every attribute, comments, and script/style/template content stay byte-identical, including attribute order and quoting. polish-apply rejects markup changes and edits that change a fact, number, unit, ticker, or required hedge, and checks cumulative edits against the original report; polish-diff applies the same rules to the whole report. An edit may not contain <, > or &, and may not touch a character reference even without them; both gates also compare the whole-document skeleton (every markup token and every character reference, byte for byte), so entities and markup are left untouched. Both gates refuse documents with markup the checker cannot model exactly. Markup changes require the full fidelity gates. Severities come from the defect registry. Nothing in the CLI calls them yet. (#11152, part of #11009)

Re-extracting a conversation no longer counts the same agreement correction again. Corrections are bound to validated user turns and recorded atomically with their replay receipts. Distinct turns on the same date still count separately; acknowledgement, drop and eviction retain replay history. Pending records from older versions use a documented agreement/date migration. (#11214, fixes #11191)

The LSP MCP server negotiates supported protocol versions. It preserves supported client versions and answers unsupported or missing versions with 2025-06-18, rather than claiming an arbitrary protocol revision. (#11208)

Skill MCP tool errors are reported as errors. Structured output and result metadata reach the caller, tool failures are not retried as connection failures, and plain content keeps its existing output format. (#11207)

The repository now has a security policy. SECURITY.md explains how to report a vulnerability privately through GitHub's Security tab, what to include, and when to expect triage and an acknowledgement; CONTRIBUTING.md links to it. Adapted from the policy proposed by @marklapointe in #10752. (#11221)

ulw-research can check that a report's sentences keep the meaning of their sources. It pairs each report sentence with the quote, claim or original text it rests on, and records drift only when two independent readers name the same kind of change; a single reader's flag is kept as a minor warning. A pair no reader judged, or a pair that needs the strong reader and only got the cheap one, is reported as unverified instead of passing as clean. Nothing in the CLI runs it yet. (#11151, part of #11009)

Skill MCP catalogs include every page. Tools, resources, and prompts follow opaque pagination cursors in order, with a clear error if a server exceeds 100 pages. (#11204)

omo update on Windows survives a package file that another process holds. When bun add -g fails with EPERM (NtSetInformationFile) because a file of the install is in use, omo update retries twice with a short backoff. If the file stays held, it names the file and the omo processes holding it, tells you to quit them and run omo update again, and keeps whatever version is installed, reinstalling the previous one only if the failed run left an unreadable install. (#10090, #11125)

A held abandoned test directory no longer fails a new run during preload. Cleanup retries through the shared tree remover, skips roots still held with EBUSY, EPERM, ENOTEMPTY or EACCES, and continues with the other abandoned roots. Unrelated filesystem errors still fail the run, and Bun's panic-only retry keeps its existing test-failure checks. (#11190, refs #11135 and #8990)

Usage summaries from older versions no longer stay on disk after an upgrade. Versions 5.1.34 to 5.1.44 kept session summaries in a rollups folder that nothing reads or sends any more. The first start after upgrading now deletes it, whether telemetry is on or off, and never follows a link out of that folder. With telemetry turned off in omo.json, a summary file that was cut short by a crash is deleted too. (#10234) (#11147) (batch #11168)

Computer-use cancellation retains engine crash diagnostics. Cancelled and timed-out calls keep their error code and carry the engine exit diagnostic as their cause. Capability-query permission failures remain structured tool results, hard engine diagnostics survive unrelated successful replies, and legacy launch refusals now share the structured record's exit-code and UTF-8 size rules. (#11150) Follow-up to #11086, part of #11073. (batch #11168)

Reflection and dream children share a machine-wide FIFO concurrency cap. The default is one running child across memory identities, configurable with memory.reflection.max_concurrent_runs. Queued runs retain their execution budget and remain protected from crash reconciliation until admitted. (#11161, fixes #11154) (batch #11168)

Memory reflection no longer stops for good when its host dies during a run. If the host died after a run had finished but before it was published, runtime/reflection/active.lock stayed held and every later reflection request only queued behind it until someone removed the file by hand. Now /reflect and every later turn settle a run whose launcher and publish claimant are both gone and whose outcome was recorded, so the queued request runs. A lock whose owner can't be checked is never reclaimed; it is reported with the next step, in the session and in omo doctor. Thanks to @Bearmancer for the report. (#10377, #10388, #10528) (batch #11168)

One bad memory entry no longer discards a whole reflection run, and credential-shaped prose is not a secret. Completion validation used to fail the entire run when any entry was refused (secret-like content, invalid frontmatter, a description over 1024 characters), so automatic reflection parked after a few runs. It now rewrites the run's branch as one commit without the refused entries, keeps the valid ones, and reports each dropped path with its reason (never the matched value); a run whose entries are all refused lands as no changes. The secret scanner and the pre-commit hook no longer flag assignments whose value only refers to a secret (process.env.X, $VAR, {{ slot }}, [FILTERED], the Bearer word, null/example literals), while a real value still matches. The dream's path_orphan finding now says when a file has no frontmatter, and its persona tells it to add a description in the same commit as the move instead of a bare git mv, which validation refused on every run. Thanks to @PGY0403 (#10385) and @floweredao (#10511) for the reports. (#10385, #10511, #10528) (batch #11168)

Held packaging-test directories are recorded without failing cleanup. The packed memory-sync probe and abandoned-root sweep share one best-effort removal guard, with no retries. Every held root is printed in the test log; Windows diagnostics are bounded to 30 seconds and include the holder's pid, image and parent pid when identified, or the diagnostic failure. Unrelated filesystem errors and unrecorded leaks still fail. (#11218, part of #11135)

rea-agents-6.8.0rea-agents: v6.8.0 Stable2026-10-25T08:36:02Z

6.8.0 (2026-10-25)

Highlights

  • Build local agent environments from network captures, browser scenarios, or a prompt, then serve derived reads and evidence-gated writes and compare them with fresh application captures.
  • Inspect more shipped formats, including GPU code objects, BEAM, Python bytecode, Unity IL2CPP metadata, Godot PCK, PyInstaller, Node.js SEA, devicetree, and additional PE/ELF tables.
  • Keep large investigations focused with retained summaries, selected fact queries, configurable recovery limits, and row-oriented CLI exports. Complete Evidence remains available.

⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

  • Function analysis: analyze_function now defaults to a retained summary. Set detail: "complete" for the previous inline dossier; the CLI retains its complete default and requires --evidence_output when selecting a summary. #5660. Thanks @CryptoJones, @gensokyo-dev, @RishTheFish21.
  • Accessibility: Accept JSON numbers and booleans in node value, alongside strings and null. Update consumers that previously assumed text-only values. cc90771, #6759, #6467. Thanks @coygeek, @gensokyo-dev, @mvanhorn.
  • JSON Lines: --format jsonl now emits object-collection rows followed by a $rea: "jsonl/v1", type: "metadata" record; scalar selections and unsupported reports are refused. Parse control records separately, select a single supported collection when necessary, and use --format json for the previous whole-document output. See JSON Lines framing. #5629, #5487. Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @jayykernel, @vijaykiran06.
  • Interface Builder: Keyed-archive graph identities use decimal table UIDs instead of authored objectID/object-id. Treat authored IDs as attributes and update persisted graph lookups; ibtool and native NIBArchive identities keep their source semantics. 6fbe67d.
  • Frida: instrument-with-frida JSON returns the canonical Evidence directly. Read normalized_result.cleanup_error instead of the former top-level wrapper field. 48f700e.
  • Reconstruction proof: ID-array proof records no longer establish verified or ready ledgers. Supply implementation/revision bindings and individual claim outcomes; handle inapplicable, unavailable, and failed proof diagnostics. f8b65e7.
  • Mach-O imports: Handle a null import name when identity is ambiguous; use raw_name and name_status to distinguish observed expressions from exact symbols. 5d0d243, 169512a.
  • Node.js: Use a runtime satisfying ^22.23.4 || ^24.22.1 || >=26.12.1; Node 23 and 25 are unsupported. Upgrade Node before installing or updating REA; the installer does not upgrade it. #6315, #6283. Thanks @GamerDubz, @gensokyo-dev, @ShikamaruNaaraa.
  • Browser storage: Historical hashed storage remains readable, but comparisons stay unknown until both captures are recaptured in the new representation. #6341, #6289. Thanks @coygeek, @gongfpp.
  • Environment serving: environment-serve serves derived state by default, including write tools whose effects are confirmed by recorded evidence. Use --replay-only for stateless replay of manifest tools. Read stop-summary state instead of state_evidence_id. #5351, #6158. Thanks @earakely-scale, @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji.
  • MCP schemas: Input advertisements default to compact. Set REA_MCP_INPUT_SCHEMA_PROFILE=full for complete advertised guidance and nested constraints. #5975. Thanks @gensokyo-dev.
  • Browser console arguments: Accept null argument_types and text_capture.status: "unavailable" when the protocol argument inventory is omitted or malformed; preserve that uncertainty. #6016, #5934. Thanks @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji.
  • Reconstruction summaries: summary.by_evidence_authority is keyed by Evidence authority; read the former source-state counts from required summary.by_source_state. Regenerate ledgers whose closure digests depended on the old projection. #5875, #5838. Thanks @coygeek, @kaoru0822-kitauji.
  • Swift bindings: Conformance type_name and protocol_name may be null; consume typed type_binding/protocol_binding evidence. Replace the former Objective-C-specific import-binding type/schema with MachoImportBinding. d0067ba.
  • WebSocket frames: Accept null opcodes and preserve unknown frame facts; numeric opcodes are restricted to the four-bit protocol range. 0bc2b42.
  • Resource-only inspection: Evidence subjects and executable digests may be null. Use resource_root and each resource's reported provenance instead of requiring an executable target. 6a6adf7.
  • Browser timestamps: Console and session-timeline timestamps may be null when the protocol supplies no finite/comparable time. Keep unknown time explicit in sorting and comparisons. 690fc51, 2a0410f.
  • Reference sources: Read failed root enumeration from rootFailure rather than a synthetic failed child at "."; preserve the top-level source-graph limitation. 6296fdf.
  • Filesystem capture: Accept entry_unavailable and identity_changed enumeration omission reasons in addition to the previous reason literals. c85b44e. Thanks @rudrakshagarwal4200-ai.
  • APK locales: Replace numeric legacy selectors such as es-r419 with es-419 or b+es+419. Two-letter regions remain supported. 29910d1. Thanks @mertkayacs.
  • XML plist scalars: Recognize $plist_type: "unknown" and read element, literal, and reason; unresolved XML scalars use this wrapper instead of the former real/null unknown marker or malformed scalar prefixes. Supported extended UTC years preserve their timestamp. 3537e58.

Changes

  • Binary layouts: Inspect ELF32 i386, little-endian ARM, and little- or big-endian MIPS EXEC/DYN/REL layouts on Linux x64 using a caller-supplied pwntools environment. #7052, #6934. Thanks @roblennon, @salmanalmutairi1.
  • Agent environments: Project network captures and Evidence files into manifests, preserve template path pairs and positions, export AgentEnv bundles, and serve manifests as replay environments. #4954, #5293, #5664, #4999, #4975. Thanks @earakely-scale, @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji.
  • Capture to environment: Build environments from browser scenarios or a prompt, extract the initial observed state, and compare the environment with application probe captures. #5182, #6946, #5319, #5699. Thanks @earakely-scale, @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji.
  • State-backed tools: Render read filters, search, sorting and paging from state; apply confirmed write effects with broader state targets and recording-based write admission. #5352, 293832b, #5800, #5787, #5600, 5375ff0, #6169, #6385, #6897. Thanks @earakely-scale, @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji.
  • State learning: Model application-assigned values, preserve view-specific clock spellings, learn write clocks from scenario timing, derive fields, and learn creates across sorted arrays and referenced entities. 7ec9b6e, #6136, 957bcfa, #6138, #6967, #7025, #7034. Thanks @earakely-scale.
  • Recorded writes: Serve creates whose compatible read-backs add omitted fields or express timestamps at a different precision, and whole-object updates that regenerate observed slugs or modification times. Conflicting observations still withhold writes. State views and bundles carrying the new read-back data need REA 6.8.0 or later to serve. #7080. Thanks @earakely-scale.
  • Native investigations: Page native inventory summaries, match functions across builds, export pseudocode to resumable directories, and select objdump disassembly by symbol. #5378, #5390, #6170, #5171, #4249, #5343, #5159. Thanks @coygeek, @CryptoJones, @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji.
  • Engine workflows: Bound a single Ghidra decompilation without closing its session, analyze GameCube/Wii DOL executables, and inspect static CUDA/HIP code objects. #5662, #6240, #5813, #5340, #5345. Thanks @arielfikru, @coygeek, @gensokyo-dev, @RishTheFish21.
  • PE/ELF: Inspect export tables without an engine, PE imports/delay-loads, Authenticode certificates, TLS callbacks/load configuration, ELF notes, Linux kernel module metadata, and Zstandard section-name tables. #6278, #6509, #4830, #7008, #4837, #7010, #6987, #4057. Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @mvanhorn, @Rinkia, @ShuaixinHuang.
  • Apple/native metadata: Inspect IPS crash reports, decode Swift nominal declarations, and select universal Mach-O slices in Go/Rust metadata readers. #6511, #6257, #6219, #5341, #4102. Thanks @coygeek, @gensokyo-dev, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @mvanhorn, @Rinkia.
  • Packaged applications: Triage desktop installation directories and inspect/extract Godot PCK, PyInstaller archives, and Node.js single-executable application blobs. #5207, #3634, afa5da7, #2433, #7000, #4399. Thanks @0xthearchitect, @coygeek, @gensokyo-dev, @mvanhorn.
  • Unity and managed code: Inventory IL2CPP images/types/methods and metadata versions 24/27; read member names and literals, link native methods and generic instances, and export Ghidra seeds. Project managed call/field-access edges and page managed member summaries. #3807, #5326, 52d1a72, #5327, #5785, #6260, #5167, #3784, #5112, #4056, #5148. Thanks @8rulerstar, @alexlanghart, @elricfd, @gensokyo-dev, @jaelliot, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @Rinkia.
  • Android: Inspect APK signing declarations and structured manifest components; report static-final DEX field values. e93e3b1, #4158, #5025, #6981, #4537, #6397. Thanks @Abelo9996, @BaaRai, @FerroLx, @gensokyo-dev, @sserrgo12.
  • Bytecode and firmware: Inspect and query CPython pyc and BEAM facts, inspect flattened devicetrees, and attribute MMIO peripheral regions in native disassembly. #4826, #6928, #4833, #6943, #7009, #5348, #6922, #6938. Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @liziye627-design, @mvanhorn.
  • JavaScript: Retain queryable static route inventories, link assignment values to writes, and accept source selections as a JSON array or file. #6019, #6206, #5029, #4772, #6193. Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @IvenKooLab, @jacobmalibiran, @JoshOsullivan-au, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @KJLavender.

Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.21.36 Stable2026-10-25T06:18:25Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.36

0.21.36 is a patch release that routes native-hook CLI error diagnostics through the configured OMX logs directory.

Native-hook CLI error logs now follow omxLogsDir: OMX_ROOT takes precedence over OMX_STATE_ROOT, and the project .omx/logs behavior is unchanged when neither is configured. This fixes #3908.

Highlights

  • NATIVE HOOK LOGGING (#3909): CLI error diagnostics use the configured omxLogsDir, honoring OMX_ROOT and OMX_STATE_ROOT instead of always writing beneath the project working directory.

Compatibility

  • No breaking API changes.
  • OMX_ROOT takes precedence over OMX_STATE_ROOT for native-hook CLI error logs; with neither configured, logs continue to use the project .omx/logs directory.

Known issues

  • #3898 (live-pin fix) remains blocked on an owner design decision; #3900 and #3907 are open drafts. On Codex 0.164.0, .omx-live-pin files in the plugin cache can invalidate hook approval. The workaround posted in #3898 disables the oh-my-codex-local plugin.

Validation

  • Pinned dev candidate 4932ef923a15a5bf818f5b4fa2625d3259486275 passed CI run 40160210335: latest-per-name checks were 17 success, 8 skipped, and 0 failures.
  • Both macOS Ralplan preflight security jobs passed: Node 20 (job 121070025433) and Node 26.5.1 (job 121070025331).
  • Release-branch local gates and release-PR review/CI are recorded in docs/qa/release-readiness-0.21.36.md.

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-10-25T05:33:01Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-1946+g659164699f
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1792527110

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

View release →

stablyai/orca ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.240 Stable2026-10-25T04:02:08Z

A more dependable workspace 🐳

This release gives the built-in browser searchable history, makes agent activity easier to follow, and improves recovery across chats and remote terminals. You’ll also find Lean and Zig syntax highlighting, support for larger quick-command collections, and a new orca ports command for finding your dev servers.

The short version

  • Conversations that stay with you: Claude chats now show turns made outside Orca. Retained messages return after reconnect without reopening the chat, and crash recovery no longer guesses that a sent message was undelivered.
  • A browser with a better memory: Search your browsing history, reopen pages, remove unwanted suggestions, and clear history with Undo. Pages can also use files you pick, save or drop through File System Access.
  • Remote work with fewer interruptions: Slow connections survive missed wake probes, reconnect notices explain what is happening, SSH terminals wait for sign-in, and new splits stay on the paired server.
  • Agent status you can trust: Completed Codex subagents stop showing as Working, running main tasks stay visible through /side conversations, and Codex MCP tools that require approval get an approval card.
  • More room for your workflow: Save more than 40 quick commands, find a worktree’s detected dev-server ports with orca ports, and reclaim old dependency installs and build output from development worktrees.
  • Polish where it counts: Lean and Zig highlighting, clearer session-search excerpts, smoother Mac trackpad scrolling in terminal apps, and better protection for unmerged branches and worktrees shared between Windows and WSL.

Phone & web updates: Updating Orca on the computer serving your phone or browser updates the served interface. This release also keeps running Codex tasks visible on paired and mobile views during /side conversations and fixes browser-picked or dropped chat attachments uploading to the paired server.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • Native chat (experimental): Update paired and SSH hosts alongside Orca for the latest chat, agent-status and launch fixes. Codex’s new approval cards cover MCP tool approvals that do not require form fields; general MCP forms and URL-based verification are not supported by this change.
  • Browser history: Open History from the page menu, or use Cmd+Y on macOS and Ctrl+H on Linux and Windows. Search, remove individual entries, or clear the last hour, today or all time, with Undo for clearing.
  • Detected ports: orca ports supports workspace selection and JSON output on runtimes with host-scoped port scanning. Direct SSH hosts do not support detection yet; native folder workspaces require --all to list the host’s listeners.
  • Development cleanup: Manual pnpm reclaim-space and automatic cleanup on development launches can reclaim ignored, untracked dependency installs and generated output in old sibling worktrees, using a default 30-day cutoff. Selected, invoking and excluded worktrees remain protected. Stop sibling apps, agents, installs, builds and tests before applying manual cleanup; reclaimed installs and output need reinstalling or rebuilding when used again.
  • Terminal file links: The earlier change to open a file link in the workspace where it was clicked was reverted in this release.

Product experience

Native chat (experimental)

Catch up on conversations, reconnect without extra steps, and answer agent prompts with clearer controls.

  • Keep sent messages from being falsely marked undelivered after a crash, and stop scanning the transcript on every reopen by @brennanb2025 in https://github.com/stablyai/orca/pull/30580
  • Show approval cards for Codex MCP tools that require permission by @nwparker in https://github.com/stablyai/orca/pull/33310

v2.51.0Reasonix CLI v2.51.0 Stable2026-10-24T22:06:05Z

CLI archives built from the Studio release studio-v2.51.0.

View release →

studio-v2.51.0Reasonix Studio v2.51.0 Stable2026-10-24T22:03:02Z

本版新增视频壁纸、查找框的大小写与正则开关、快捷键一览和长对话分页加载，正文栏可拖得更窄，并修复多处保存、粘贴与显示问题。

新增

  • 壁纸可以选 MP4 或 WebM 视频，作为静音循环的动态壁纸；窗口隐藏或失焦、系统减弱动效或精简效果下停在第一帧。关联 #14357。
  • 对话里的查找框多了两个开关：区分大小写，以及把查询当作正则表达式。关联 #14198 by @imcreazy。
  • 设置的外观页增加「快捷键」一栏，列出窗口里所有可用按键，可以搜「快捷键」「Ctrl」「Esc」等词找到。关联 #14198 by @imcreazy。
  • agent 浏览器的网页可在「设置 → 外观」开启空闲休眠：15 分钟无人使用后整页收起省内存，再用时恢复为同一标签页。默认关闭。关联 #14332 by @jf200s。
  • 版本列表里的下载和后台自动下载现在都可以取消；取消会停止传输，并删除这次下载留下的部分文件。关联 #14338。
  • 新增可选的历史接口，按页读取正文并保留稳定的消息位置和跨页工具上下文；现有客户端继续全量读取。关联 #14048、#14087 by @SivanCola。

变更

  • 壁纸不再有 12 MB 上限，原样使用你选的图片；上传改为边收边写入磁盘，读取支持分段，大文件不再占满内存。关联 #13980。
  • 正文字宽现在可以连续拖到 640px，不再停在「适宜阅读」的 760px；按 Home 或不拖动时仍是 760px。关联 #14384。
  • 阅读栏左右两侧的拖动把手平时不再显示，鼠标移到栏边缘才出现，拖动和键盘调整不变。关联 #14352 fixed in #14353。
  • 移除工作台的「加宽」按钮、独立加宽模式及 Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+B；可拖动分隔条或聚焦后用方向键调宽。关联 #14324 fixed in #14335 by @lancy69。
  • 轮次多的对话里，右侧提问细条会把相邻几条折成一组，悬停显示涵盖的范围；右键某条可加书签。关联 #14198 by @imcreazy。
  • 「运行分析」入口变成图标加文字，分析页标题左侧多了返回箭头；点已在屏上的会话会回到正文。关联 #14198 by @imcreazy。
  • 导出会话多了 Markdown 格式：只保留提问与回答，按发生顺序；JSON 仍保留全部字段。关联 #14198 by @imcreazy。
  • 开启「派发」后，把任务派到侧边栏里你自己的项目时，不再因该项目尚未决定信任而停在信任卡上。关联 #13341。
  • 没有选过项目也没有记住的项目时，首次启动不再打开用户主目录，而是打开应用自己管理的一个小目录，它不会被记成项目。关联 #14363。
  • 输入 / 后可继续输入描述中的关键词查找技能和命令；名称匹配仍优先于描述匹配。关联 #14323 fixed in #14344 by @lancy69。
  • 工具参数缺必填项、待办列表规则不符、diff 证据被拒时，报错会直接给出合法示例或改用 files 证据的出路。关联 #14311。
  • 支持分页的服务端优先显示最近消息；读取旧页保留阅读位置和控件，搜索会提示历史覆盖不完整。关联 #14048、#14087 by @SivanCola。

修复

  • 切回「发送反馈」标签或点击反馈窗口标题后，Ctrl+V 也能添加截图；打开窗口后立刻粘贴也不再被偶尔忽略。关联 #14205 fixed in #14333 by @lancy69、#14333 by @lancy69。
  • 新对话第一次保存前在右侧提问细条上加的书签，保存后不再消失，重启后也不会出现在另一段新对话里。关联 #14296 by @imcreazy。
  • 接口报告「缓存命中 0」时按完全未命中计算，缓存命中率不再虚高；异常的超额命中被截断，99.5% 以上不再写成 100%。关联 #14313 by @jf200s。
  • 后台任务启动时的提示按界面语言显示为「后台任务已开始：…」，不再是唯一的英文。关联 #14308。
  • 正文字宽的两个手柄在第一次拖过之后不再失效；拖动时两侧栏按各自比例让位，输入框跟随正文变宽。关联 #14320 by @imcreazy。
  • 展开待办计划时，右侧消息细条和「跳到最新 / 回到顶部」按钮不再被顶起；计划面板浮在输入框上方。关联 #14321 by @imcreazy。
  • 上下文压缩完成后的诊断行按界面语言写成一句话，不再是英文 key=value。关联 #14307。
  • 工作台右键菜单合并编辑操作与「加入对话」；阅读和只读视图不显示修改操作，未选中文字时不显示引用。关联 #14326 fixed in #14348 by @lancy69。
  • 还没确认的提问卡片滚出视野再回来后，已勾选的选项、自填文字、补充说明和所在题目会保留。关联 #14336 fixed in #14337。
  • 用智能体预设建的会话，升级后因内置工具变化不再变成只能查看；按当时记录的工具继续，并提示一次。关联 #14360 fixed in #14370。
  • 执行过程那一行的「N 项运行中 / 失败 / 操作」和标题现在字号、字重一致。关联 #14364。
  • 自动命名时标题模型把输出额度都花在思考上，程序会放宽额度重试一次，而不是直接失败。关联 #14374。
  • 设置「存储」里「导入原生会话」的卡片不再露出浏览器自带的文件选择框，改用应用风格按钮。关联 #14378。
  • 子代理以及计划者、Guardian 的文件读取各用独立记录，不再刷新执行代理的过期文件保护而覆盖你的修改。关联 #14025、#14133 by @SivanCola。
  • Guardian 自身的 use_capability 调用对真实目标做只读检查，拒绝写入和已声明的宿主变更。关联 #14025、#14133 by @SivanCola。
  • 重启后 Git 暂时不可用时保留隔离结果的文件和 ID；恢复前核对托管目录与仓库身份，错配的记录保留且不会被清理或应用。关联 #14025、#14133 by @SivanCola。
  • 乐观模式下的任务写入会检查兄弟任务占用，包含动态本地工具；不相交的写入仍可并行。关联 #14025、#14133 by @SivanCola。

升级须知

  • 派发到侧边栏里没决定过信任的项目时，该项目会被记为已信任并直接发出任务；家目录等不可整体信任的文件夹仍会询问。关联 #13341。
  • 乐观模式下，同一文件被兄弟任务占用时，后到的写入会以 write.path_busy 被拒绝，而不再静默覆盖。关联 #14025。

贡献者

感谢本版本的贡献者：@imcreazy、@jf200s、@SivanCola、@lancy69

<!-- lang:en -->

This release adds video wallpapers, case and regex switches in find, a shortcut sheet and paged loading of long conversations, lets the reading column go narrower, and fixes several saving, pasting and display problems.

New

  • A wallpaper can be an MP4 or WebM video, played muted and looping; it stays on its first frame while the window is hidden or unfocused, under reduced motion or reduced effects. Refs #14357.
  • The find bar gained two switches: match case, and read the query as a regular expression. Refs #14198 by @imcreazy.
  • The appearance settings gained a keyboard shortcut sheet listing every chord the window answers to, reachable by searching "shortcut", "Ctrl" or "Esc". Refs #14198 by @imcreazy.
  • Agent browser pages can sleep when idle (Settings → Appearance): a page nobody drives for 15 minutes is folded away and wakes as the same tab. Off by default. Refs #14332 by @jf200s.
  • A version download, started from the version list or by automatic download, can now be cancelled; cancelling stops the transfer and removes the partial files. Refs #14338.
  • An optional history API reads the transcript in bounded pages with stable positions and cross-page tool context; existing clients keep full reads. Refs #14048, #14087 by @SivanCola.

Changed

  • Wallpapers have no 12 MB limit and are used as chosen; the upload is written to disk as it arrives and served in ranges, so a large one no longer fills memory. Refs #13980.
  • The reading column can be dragged continuously down to 640px instead of stopping at 760px; Home and an untouched window still give 760px. Refs #14384.
  • The reading column's resize grips no longer draw at rest; they appear under the pointer or on keyboard focus, and dragging and keys are unchanged. Refs #14352 fixed in #14353.
  • Removed Widen mode, its button and Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+B; use the divider or the arrow keys on it to resize. Refs #14324 fixed in #14335 by @lancy69.
  • A long conversation folds neighbouring questions in the rail into one line and names the range on hover; right click stars one. Refs #14198 by @imcreazy.
  • The run analysis entry reads as words beside its icon, the page has a back arrow, and picking a conversation already on screen returns to reading. Refs #14198 by @imcreazy.
  • A conversation can be exported as Markdown, keeping only the questions and answers in order; JSON still carries every field. Refs #14198 by @imcreazy.
  • With dispatch on, a task sent to a project in your sidebar no longer stops on its trust card because you hadn't decided about it yet. Refs #13341.
  • With no chosen or remembered project, a first launch opens a small folder the app owns instead of your home directory; it is never remembered as a project. Refs #14363.
  • Type description keywords after / to find skills and commands; name matches still rank first. Refs #14323 fixed in #14344 by @lancy69.
  • A tool call missing a required field, a todo list breaking a rule, and a diff citation for command-made changes now show a valid example or the files-evidence way out. Refs #14311.
  • Paging servers show the latest messages first; older pages keep your reading position and controls, and search says when history is incomplete. Refs #14048, #14087 by @SivanCola.

Fixed

  • Pasting a screenshot works after returning to the Send feedback tab or clicking the dialog heading, and a paste right after opening is no longer sometimes ignored. Refs #14205 fixed in #14333 by @lancy69, #14333 by @lancy69.
  • A question starred in the rail before a new conversation is first saved stays starred, and no longer reappears in another unsaved conversation after a restart. Refs #14296 by @imcreazy.
  • A request reported with zero cache hits counts as a full miss, so the hit rate no longer drifts up; over-reported hits are clamped and 99.5% no longer shows as 100%. Refs #14313 by @jf200s.
  • The notice shown when a background job starts is worded in the interface language instead of staying the one English line. Refs #14308.
  • The reading column's grips no longer stop after the first drag; sidebars give way while dragging and the composer follows the width. Refs #14320 by @imcreazy.
  • Opening the todo plan no longer pushes the message rail or the jump buttons up; the panel floats above the composer. Refs #14321 by @imcreazy.
  • The line shown after a context fold is one sentence in the interface language, not an English key=value dump. Refs #14307.
  • Workbench text menus combine editing and Add to conversation, respect read-only views and selection, and keep focus and undo/redo. Refs #14326 fixed in #14348 by @lancy69.
  • An unanswered ask card keeps its picks, typed text, notes and current question when it scrolls out of view and back. Refs #14336 fixed in #14337.
  • A conversation bound to an Agent preset no longer turns read-only after an upgrade changes a built-in tool; it continues with the recorded tools and says so once. Refs #14360 fixed in #14370.
  • The running / failed / action counts on the activity line now share the title's size and weight. Refs #14364.
  • When the title model spends its whole output budget thinking, automatic naming asks once more with room for the title instead of failing. Refs #14374.
  • The "Import a native session" card in Settings → Storage no longer shows the browser's own file input; it uses an app-styled button. Refs #14378.
  • Reads by child tasks, the Planner and the Guardian use their own records and no longer refresh the executor's stale-file guard and overwrite your edits. Refs #14025, #14133 by @SivanCola.
  • The Guardian's own use_capability calls enforce read-only execution on the real target, refusing writes and declared host mutations. Refs #14025, #14133 by @SivanCola.
  • After a restart with Git unavailable, an isolated result keeps its files and ID; recovery checks managed paths and repository identity, and a mismatched record stays untouched. Refs #14025, #14133 by @SivanCola.
  • Optimistic task writes honour live sibling claims, including dynamically resolved local tools; disjoint writes stay parallel. Refs #14025, #14133 by @SivanCola.

Upgrade notes

  • Dispatching to a sidebar project you have not decided about records it as trusted and sends the task; folders that cannot be trusted whole, such as your home directory, still ask. Refs #13341.
  • In optimistic mode, a write to a file a sibling task holds is refused with write.path_busy instead of silently overwriting it. Refs #14025.

Contributors

Thanks to this release's contributors: @imcreazy, @jf200s, @SivanCola, @lancy69

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。x86_64 用文件名带 amd64 的包，aarch64（统信 UOS、麒麟等）用带 arm64 的包。也提供 .AppImage（只提供下载，不会自更新；需要 FUSE 2，或用 --appimage-extract-and-run 启动）。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

Feature Description By
Kimi support (off by default) Folders, Conversation export, Comfortable Reading Width, Reading Typography, Ctrl/⌘+Enter to send, Draft auto-save, Draft counter, Quote replies and Site check now run on Kimi; turn on the ones you want under Plugins. (closes #1038) —
Folders stay with their own account With account isolation on, folders, Drive sync and Folder as Project's instructions follow the Google account you're on; an unreadable account is reported instead of loading forever. —
Gemini: new chat in a folder without a reload With Folder as Project on, New chat in this folder opens a new chat right away with that folder picked. —
Site check (off by default) See how many of the page elements Voyager relies on were found, and pick the part that looks broken; the report has no chat content and is ready for an issue. —
Claude and DeepSeek export (off by default) Turn on Conversation export under Plugins to save a whole conversation to a file, read from start to finish. —
Voyager on phones On Firefox for Android, the timelines, Gemini and ChatGPT export and the visual effects work; other plugins say they aren't available on phones yet. (closes #1066) PR #1068 by @ccch1mneyyy
Parallel Local Bridge jobs (off by default) Several Voyager CLI jobs run at once, each in its own ChatGPT tab (CLI 0.5.0 or later). PR #1067 by @ccch1mneyyy

Claude's redesigned sidebar Voyager finds Claude's sidebar again after its redesign. —
Local Bridge model switching Voyager CLI reports a model switch only once the menu has closed and the model really changed. —
Firefox sakura effect No longer shows a patch of background on dark pages. —

📥 Installation

  • Chrome / Edge / Chromium browsers: voyager-chrome-v2.2.1.zip
  • Firefox: voyager-firefox-v2.2.1.xpi
  • Safari: voyager-v2.2.1.dmg

Do not use GitHub's auto-generated Source code (zip/tar.gz) archives for manual extension installs. Those files are repository source snapshots, not browser-ready extension packages.

<details> <summary><b>🍎 Safari 安装与升级说明 (Safari installation & upgrade)</b></summary>

🎉 从 v1.6.0 起，Voyager 的核心功能已正式覆盖 Safari。 Starting with v1.6.0, Voyager's core experience is officially supported on Safari.

  • 📦 安装 (Installation): 下载并打开 voyager-v2.2.1.dmg，按提示安装应用。 Download and open voyager-v2.2.1.dmg, then follow the prompts to install the app.
  • ⚠️ 旧版升级 (Legacy upgrade): 如果仍安装着 Gemini Voyager.app，请先完成一次 Safari 迁移步骤，避免两个 App 并存。 If Gemini Voyager.app is still installed, follow the one-time Safari migration guide first so the two apps do not conflict.
  • 🖼️ 图片能力 (Images): Image Refinement 水印去除、全尺寸图片下载与聊天导出中的图片提取均已支持。 Image Refinement watermark removal, full-size image downloads, and image extraction in conversation exports are supported.
  • ☁️ 原生云同步 (Native cloud sync): Google Drive 与 iCloud 同步均已接入 Safari 原生层。 Google Drive and iCloud sync are integrated through Safari's native layer.
  • 🔔 系统通知 (Notifications): Gemini 回复完成后可发送 macOS 通知，并直达对应对话。 macOS can notify you when a Gemini response finishes and open the matching conversation.
  • 🔄 自动更新 (Automatic updates): 完成这次迁移后，后续版本可通过 Sparkle 自动更新。 After this one-time migration, future versions can update automatically through Sparkle.

</details>

codewhale-hq/Codewhale ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.10.2Codewhale v0.10.2 Stable2026-10-24T17:31:14Z

What's in v0.10.2

Codewhale v0.10.2 adds an animated pet view (/pet on), a live Terminal work dock, per-chat composer colors (/color) and a one-time note on the first start after an update that says what changed. It closes three session data-loss paths and a codewhale web session leak; adds the default Runtime daemon socket locations to the sandbox deny-list; holds a curl or wget that uploads a credential; refuses shell commands containing invisible Unicode; keeps the Linux sandbox working when a hidden credential directory is a symlink; and reduces idle TUI redraw work. /undo takes back a whole request, including long and multiline prompts. exec --auto explains its Full Access default, honors an explicitly stricter sandbox, and saves tool-enabled runs and their failure receipts. Local runtimes appear during onboarding, /diff works outside a Git repository, DeepSeek's Responses route no longer rejects replayed tool history, and contributor fixes improve token accounting, relay model lists, localized replies and Windows launcher guidance. Delegated work continues without model-invented turn or time caps, and independent workflow steps can continue after another child fails.

Install

Recommended — official GitHub release

New macOS/Linux install (checksummed binaries from this release):

curl -fsSL https://codewhale.net/install.sh | CODEWHALE_VERSION="v0.10.2" sh
"$HOME/.local/bin/codewhale" --version

For Windows, use the matching installer or archive below. For an existing direct install, run codewhale update; it prints the executable path and keeps newer builds. If the install directory is occupied by a different build, use the fresh-directory migration in the installation guide.

Secondary packaging — npm and Cargo

npm install -g codewhale
# or build from source
cargo install codewhale-cli --locked

The wrapper downloads the matched codewhale and codew command assets from this Release. Both contain the same compiled runtime.

Docker / GHCR

docker run --rm -it \
  -e DEEPSEEK_API_KEY="$DEEPSEEK_API_KEY" \
  -v codewhale-home:/home/codewhale/.codewhale \
  ghcr.io/codewhale-hq/codewhale:v0.10.2

The image exposes the same runtime as both codewhale and codew. The latest tag is also updated on release.

Cargo (Linux / macOS)

cargo install codewhale-cli --locked

The Cargo package installs codewhale. Cargo cannot create a second command alias from one binary target; users who want the shorter spelling can add a codew symlink to that installed executable. The npm, Homebrew, archive, shell-installer, and container channels install both command names directly.

Manual download — platform archives (recommended)

Each archive below contains the same runtime under the codewhale and codew command names, plus an install script:

Platform Archive Install script
Linux x64 codewhale-linux-x64.tar.gz install.sh
Linux ARM64 codewhale-linux-arm64.tar.gz install.sh
Android ARM64 (Termux) codewhale-android-arm64.tar.gz install.sh
macOS x64 codewhale-macos-x64.tar.gz install.sh
macOS ARM codewhale-macos-arm64.tar.gz install.sh
Windows x64 (installer) CodeWhaleSetup.exe NSIS setup
Windows x64 codewhale-windows-x64.zip install.bat
Windows x64 (portable) codewhale-windows-x64-portable.zip —
Windows ARM64 codewhale-windows-arm64.zip install.bat
Windows ARM64 (portable) codewhale-windows-arm64-portable.zip —

Unix (Linux / macOS):

tar xzf codewhale-<platform>.tar.gz
cd codewhale-<platform>
./install.sh

Windows:

  • For the installer path, run CodeWhaleSetup.exe; it installs codewhale.exe, codew.exe, and codewhale.bat under %LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\CodeWhale\bin, adds that directory to the current-user PATH, and creates a Start Menu shortcut that prefers Windows Terminal (wt.exe) when it is installed.
  • Extract the archive for your machine: codewhale-windows-x64.zip or codewhale-windows-arm64.zip
  • Double-click codewhale.bat (not the raw .exe) to launch
  • Run install.bat to copy the binaries and launcher to %USERPROFILE%\bin
  • Add %USERPROFILE%\bin to your PATH

The portable Windows archive skips the install script — extract and run codewhale.bat from any directory. The NSIS installer is currently unsigned and may trigger Windows SmartScreen until a signing certificate is wired into the release pipeline.

Each platform also has bare, unarchived codewhale-<platform> and codew-<platform> assets. The seven codewhale-tui-<platform> filenames attached to v0.9.5 are byte-identical compatibility copies used only to let already-installed v0.9.4 clients discover and cross this single-binary transition; current installers do not expose a third runtime. The legacy npm package deepseek-tui is deprecated and is not republished. For migration from v0.8.x legacy binary names, see docs/REBRAND.md.

Verify (recommended)

Download the checksum manifests from this Release and verify:

# Linux — archive bundles
sha256sum -c codewhale-bundles-sha256.txt --ignore-missing

# Linux — individual binaries
sha256sum -c codewhale-artifacts-sha256.txt --ignore-missing

# macOS
shasum -a 256 -c codewhale-bundles-sha256.txt --ignore-missing
shasum -a 256 -c codewhale-artifacts-sha256.txt --ignore-missing

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.20CC Switch v4.0.20 Stable2026-10-24T15:32:07Z

CC Switch v4.0.20

本版本新增对 Oh My Pi 的支持，它是 CC Switch 管理的第 13 个应用：多个供应商可以同时写进它的 models.yml，MCP、Skills、提示词同步给 Oh My Pi，会话可以浏览和恢复，「应用」页可以安装、升级、卸载 Oh My Pi。「应用」页分成「已安装」「未安装」两组，已安装的应用可以拖动排序，侧栏跟着这个顺序。Kimi 官方账号的卡片现在可以编辑模型、从 Kimi Code 里移除；托管的 ChatGPT 账号可以用浏览器登录。另外修复了 DeepSeek 余额用完后显示成「套餐已过期」、Kimi Code 子代理的用量没有统计、Skills 更新一直报 HTTP 429、Pi 的 API Key 写成环境变量引用时额度查询 401、Claude Code 会话标题显示成 <local-command-stdout>、Linux 上开启应用级窗口按钮后页头拖不动等问题。4.0 完整的更新内容请见 v4.0 发布说明。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

Oh My Pi

Oh My Pi（命令是 omp）现在是一个独立的应用，有自己的供应商、MCP、Skills、提示词和会话（#6747、#8308）。配置在 ~/.omp/agent，用了命名 profile 时在 ~/.omp/profiles/<名称>/agent；也可以在设置里改成别的目录。

  • 供应商可以同时启用。 和 Pi 一样，卡片上是「启用」「移除」，多个供应商同时写进 models.yml，在 Oh My Pi 里选用。只改 CC Switch 写的那一条，文件里其他内容原样保留；用了 YAML 锚点、标签这类写法的条目不会被重写，会提示你手动修改。
  • 写入前按 Oh My Pi 的规则检查。 Oh My Pi 只要有一条供应商不合规，就会停用所有自定义供应商，所以填了它不接受的值，保存时会直接提示，不会写进去。只有旧版的 models.json、还没有 models.yml 时，会提示你先运行一次 omp，让它自己迁移，不会新建文件挤掉你原来的供应商。
  • 添加供应商。 表单和 Pi 共用，预设也来自 Pi 的目录；「获取模型列表」后在弹窗里勾选模型。「模型自动发现」（让 Oh My Pi 自己去接口查有哪些模型）和请求头、兼容选项一起放在「高级选项」里。API Key 可以写环境变量名，查询额度时 CC Switch 会按 Oh My Pi 的规则取出实际的值。
  • MCP、Skills、提示词。 MCP 写进 mcp.json，Skills 装进 Oh My Pi 自己的技能目录，提示词写进 AGENTS.md。Oh My Pi 还会从其他工具的目录里找 MCP 和技能，实际加载的可能比 CC Switch 里列出的多，MCP 和 Skills 页 Oh My Pi 一列的说明里有解释。
  • 会话。 可以浏览和恢复 Oh My Pi 的会话，预览里能显示项目里的图片。
  • 「应用」页管理 Oh My Pi。 用官方安装脚本安装（装了 Bun 就用 Bun，没装就下载官方的二进制），升级运行 omp update（用 Homebrew 装的仍然走 Homebrew），可以卸载。
  • Oh My Pi 不经过本地路由，也暂不统计用量。

托管 ChatGPT 账号可以用浏览器登录

在授权中心或 Codex 的供应商表单里添加托管 ChatGPT 账号时，可以在「登录方式」里选「浏览器登录」（#8288、#8284）。有些企业工作区关掉了设备代码登录，这些账号以前加不进来；重新登录也可以用浏览器。「设备代码登录」仍然可选。

改进

「应用」页分成「已安装」「未安装」两组。 每组标题带数量，没有应用的组不显示；刚打开、还在检测时先显示一整张表，检测完再分组，行不会一个个跳。Claude Desktop 没有命令可查，按是否装了 Desktop 来分组，行里照旧写是否已接入 CC Switch。

已安装的应用可以拖动排序。 鼠标移到某一行上，行首会出现拖动手柄，和供应商卡片排序一样。侧栏和默认打开的应用都跟着这个顺序；顺序只存在本机，不随云同步。

Kimi 官方账号的卡片可以编辑和移除。 以前官方卡片只能在授权中心管理，模型停在登录时的那一份。现在可以编辑卡片：连接和登录仍由授权中心管，这里只改模型，「获取模型列表」读取这个账号当前可用的官方模型，带上官方的上下文窗口、能力和思考档位。官方卡片也可以像其他 Kimi 供应商一样从 Kimi Code 里「移除」，卡片和登录都保留，再点「添加」就恢复；要删除，先移除，或者到授权中心删。

没有 Kimi Code 套餐的 Kimi 账号显示「未订阅」。 以前授权中心和官方卡片的额度一栏是空的，看起来像查询失败。

余额用完也显示金额。 余额为零或欠费时，供应商卡片和托盘以前只写「余额已用完」，看不出欠了多少，红字也和「额度查询失败」分不清。现在一律显示「余额 <金额>」，为零或负数时卡片上整行变红、托盘里变成橙色，和以前一样。

额度查询失败的中文提示改成「额度查询失败」（以前是「额度没查到」），供应商卡片、授权中心和托盘一起改。

Pi 获取模型列表后在弹窗里勾选。 以前只能逐行从下拉框里选，现在和 OpenCode、Codex、Kimi Code 一样弹出列表，勾选的模型自动加成一行，从预设或 models.dev 补上参数；取消勾选会删掉对应的行。逐行的下拉框还在。

Pi 的请求头和兼容选项收进「高级选项」。 默认收起，和 Claude Code、Codex 的表单一致；兼容选项的说明改成了白话。

Claude Code、Claude Desktop 选 GitHub Copilot 供应商时，上游格式显示为「由路由按模型自动选择」（#8196、#8195）。Copilot 的上游格式本来就按模型自动选，以前表单里还能手选，选了也不生效。

用量页明细页签的数量一次全显示。 以前「模型」页签要点开才有数量，切换应用时三个数量会消失再出现，页签栏跟着跳。

价格表新增 Kimi K3.1 Pro 和 K3.1 Pro HighSpeed。

土耳其语翻译补全（#8616）。

预设： 各家预设的模型更新到当前的最新型号，填上 Key 就能用。

修复

  • DeepSeek 余额为零或欠费时，卡片和托盘显示「套餐已过期」，余额数字不见了（#8812）。DeepSeek 返回的「是否可用」只表示余额够不够调用，余额一到零就变成否，Key 本身仍然有效。现在照常显示余额；Key 真的失效时，仍按认证失败提示。
  • Kimi Code 子代理的用量没有统计进来（#8792）。Kimi Code 把每个子代理的记录写在单独的文件里，以前只读了主代理的那份。现在每个子代理都会导入，按每次调用记账，不会和主代理的汇总重复计算。
  • Antigravity 子代理和 ACP 会话的用量记成未知模型（#8693）。现在从 agy 本地的对话数据库里找回模型（只读，不会等 agy 写完）。
  • Skills 安装或更新一直报 HTTP 429（#8813）。下载 GitHub 仓库压缩包时没带 User-Agent，GitHub 对这类请求限流更严，多人共用一个出口 IP 时更容易碰到。现在会带上 CC-Switch/<version>。
  • Pi 的 API Key 写成 $NAME、${NAME} 这类环境变量引用时，额度和余额查询报 401（#8822）。以前把引用原样当成 Key 发出去，现在按 Pi 的规则取出变量的值；变量没设置时，也不会再把引用当成 Key 发出去。!command 形式的 Key，CC Switch 仍然不会执行。取出的值只用于这次查询，不写回配置。
  • Claude Code 会话在会话管理里的标题显示成 <local-command-stdout>Set model to opus</local-command-stdout>（#8799、#8798）。会话一开头运行了 /model、/effort 这类本地命令时，标题会这样；最后运行了本地命令时，列表里显示的最后一条消息也是命令输出。现在会跳过这些记录。
  • Linux 上开启「启用应用级窗口按钮」后，页头拖不动窗口，窗口边缘也没有缩放光标（#8092、#8091）。
  • Codex 经 Chat 上游时，带图片的孤立工具结果里，占位文字说图片在「下一条消息」里，实际并没有这条消息（#8157）。现在改成说图片已经作为图片附上。

升级说明

数据库版本从 22 升到 23，为 MCP 增加 Oh My Pi 的开关。升级前会自动备份数据库；升级后再退回旧版本，旧版本会提示数据库版本过新，打不开。

  • Oh My Pi 默认显示在侧栏，不用的话可以在「应用」页关掉「在侧栏显示 Oh My Pi」。
